# Test cases

For anyone changing the code: one table per test suite. **Checked by** points at what checks the case, as `` `file#text` ``:
the text is the test's name or the line it prints, and `cli/tests/test_repo.py` fails when either is gone. **Stage** says where
it runs ([testing.md](testing.md)):

- `ui`: CI's `contract` and `ui` jobs, on a push to main or a pull request: the game server's contract and an upgrade from the last
  release, then the pages in three parts, on stand-in files (`tests/game/protocol.sh`, `tests/upgrade/run.sh`, `tests/ui/run.sh`)
- `unit`: another CI job, without the game's files (`changes`, `test`, `images`, `arm32`, `host`, `client`, or a Windows job:
  `arm32-windows`, `arm32-windows-wine`, `arm32-windows-real`, `client-build`, `client-windows`, `windows-bundle`, `windows`)
- `qa`: on a machine with the game's files (`tests/qa.sh`)
- `hand`: by a person, on a device

## TS-PAGE: the public pages

| ID | Scenario | Expected | Checked by | Stage |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---:|
| PAGE-01 | A browser set to Korean opens the site for the first time | English: the language follows the visitor's choice only | `tests/ui/audit.js#a browser set to Korean first sees English` | ui |
| PAGE-02 | The visitor picks a language | The page changes, and the choice stays | `tests/ui/audit.js#the language switch changes the page` | ui |

## TS-LIVE: broadcasts and replays

| ID | Scenario | Expected | Checked by | Stage |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---:|
| LIVE-01 | A player the owner has not switched on | No upload key, no notice | `tests/live/smoke.py#off by default: no token, no notice` | qa |
| LIVE-02 | Two players on air | The video runs, marked LIVE; both listed; pages show who is on | `tests/ui/audit.js#the broadcast plays` | ui |
| LIVE-03 | The pages while players are on air | No layout findings, at a phone's and a laptop's width | `tests/ui/audit.js#const AIR` | ui |
| LIVE-04 | A recording | Plays, marked REPLAY, with its timeline | `tests/ui/audit.js#a recording plays` | ui |

## TS-GAME: the patched client

| ID | Scenario | Expected | Checked by | Stage |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---:|
| GAME-01 | Every code edit on the real clients | Finds its bytes in the client's own version, and no other version's | `tools/tests/test_real_client.py#test_every_code_edit_fits_in_the_clients_own_version` | qa |

| SRV-52 | A chat that does not take a message | Tried again after a wait (10 s, then longer), the webhook's later messages after it in their order; a chat that asks to wait (429) waits as asked and it is no try, given up and counted half an hour after its news; a 404 stops it and says the webhook is not there; given up and counted half an hour after what it tells, and not sent at all when found older than that; sent at most once, also when the process stopped after the chat took it; no answer in time and no chat there said apart; a redirect not followed | `tools/tests/test_webhooks.py#class SendingTest` | unit |
| SRV-53 | A restore with webhooks, on the admin pages and with `./opendmtq restore` | Today's webhooks stay (not the backup's, not even on a server in use with none; a server with nothing of its own yet takes the backup's, ADM-171), the restored loop's memory and queue are gone, and the next look sends nothing of the restored data | `tools/tests/test_webhooks.py#class RestoreTest`, `cli/tests/test_backup.py#test_a_restore_keeps_todays_webhooks_and_sends_nothing_of_the_backups`, `cli/tests/test_backup.py#test_a_restore_on_a_server_with_players_and_no_webhooks_brings_none_back`, `cli/tests/test_backup.py#test_the_webhooks_tables_are_the_ones_the_admin_pages_make` | unit |

## TS-DEPLOY: packaging and releases

| ID | Scenario | Expected | Checked by | Stage |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---:|
